Williams helped to develop, in Pasadena and elsewhere, the style we now refer to as “Spanish Colonial Revival”. Presenter and Board Member Dave Nufer will examine the many notable Spanish Colonial Revival style buildings in Pasadena including City Hall, the Central Library, the Pasadena Playhouse, and the historic Caltech campus. A study of Pasadena’s recent Hispanic-influenced projects, such as the Del Mar Station complex and the Paseo, will be included as well. Join us as we trace the thousand-year development of Spanish architecture from its Moorish roots to its revival and prominence in Pasadena today. \n \n\nPrevious Pasadena Now story:\n\nThe Pasadena Public Library hosts Dave Nufer, program developer and docent with Pasadena Heritage and the Los Angeles Conservancy, in a virtual webinar from 4 to 5 p.m. Wednesday, where he explains how Spanish-influenced architecture has become one of the greatest Latinx contributions to American culture.\nNufer will also present how Hispanic architecture — with millions of examples from California to Florida built by tens of millions of people over the last 400 years  —  has helped shape Pasadena’s cityscape.\nThe presentation focuses on how the Spanish/Hispanic/Latinx architectural design vocabulary evolved over 1,000 years on four continents, and how it has been employed in Pasadena. It examines numerous local examples, including the San Gabriel Mission, Castle Green, the Caltech campus, the Civic Center, the 1920s Spanish Colonial Revival houses built by George Washington Smith, Wallace Neff, and many others, along with more recent Hispanic inflected projects such as the Del Mar Station.\nDave Nufer has previously given talks at the Pasadena Public Library on the architecture, history, and cultural contributions of other Pasadena ethnic communities. (AP) — The profound impact of Hispanic architecture on Pasadena's cityscape was explored in a well-attended event Thursday, as part of the city's Hispanic Heritage Month celebrations.\n\nRenowned presenter Dave Nufer guided attendees through a journey across time, uncovering the birth of the Spanish Colonial Revival style in the early 20th century. Iconic landmarks such as City Hall, the Central Library, and Caltech's historic campus were showcased, along with contemporary Hispanic-influenced projects such as the Del Mar Station complex and the Paseo.\n\nKristen Norton, Education Director for Pasadena Heritage, emphasized the importance of recognizing and celebrating the Hispanic architectural influences in the community. \"Pasadena Heritage, like many other preservation groups and organizations across the country, really recognizes the need to create more inclusive programming and to foster stronger connections with the communities that they represent,\" she said.\n\nNufer, a presenter and member of the Pasadena Heritage Board of Directors and Education Committee, highlighted the contributions of architects like Myron Hunt, Wallace Neff, Bertram Goodhue, and Paul R. Williams in developing the Spanish Colonial Revival style in Pasadena and beyond.\n\nThe event aimed to foster an appreciation for these resources and ensure their preservation for future generations to enjoy. \"Our mission over these last plus years has been to promote the preservation of Pasadena's resources and advocacy and also education,\" Norton said.\n\nThe Spanish Colonial Revival style, born in the early 20th century, saw the combination of red tile roofs, white stucco walls, arcades, decorative iron work, and colorful polychromatic tiles in houses that created lush, Mediterranean, paradises, defining Pasadena and Southern California for the world visually, then and to this day.\n\nThe Pasadena Public Library hosted Nufer in a virtual webinar earlier this month, where he explained how Spanish-influenced architecture has become one of the greatest Latinx contributions to American culture and shaped Pasadena's cityscape."}, "messages": [{"role": "system", "content": "请根据task和提供的评分标准，逐项核验当前这份回答是否满足要求。\n每一项都应针对其具体标准作出判断，不能补造事实，也不要猜测哪份回答更受偏好。\n\n对每个criterion_id返回一次判断，并在reason中说明依据，避免用占位词代替解释。\n满足该项要求时，satisfied填写JSON布尔值true；不满足时填写false，不使用字符串或其他取值。\n字段约束：仅criterion_judgments数组，每项仅criterion_id、satisfied、reason；satisfied仅true/false，reason非空。\n严格只返回以下字段的JSON，示意值必须替换：{\"criterion_judgments\": [{\"criterion_id\": \"提供的编号\", \"satisfied\": true, \"reason\": \"具体判断理由\"}]}"}, {"role": "user", "content": "{\"task\": \"rewrite this information into a proper news story in Associated Press inverted pyramid style: In an Thursday, Sept. 28, the profound impact of Hispanic architecture on Pasadena's cityscape was explored.
